Look for a row that includes a Phase label and a Ductwork Demand label\n    has_phase = any(isinstance(v, str) and v.strip().lower() == 'phase' for v in row_vals)\n    has_demand = any(isinstance(v, str) and 'ductwork demand' in v.lower() for v in row_vals)\n    if has_phase and has_demand:\n        header_row = r\n        break\n\nif header_row is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(\"Could not find header row with 'Phase' and 'Ductwork Demand'\")\n\nfor c in range(1, ws_demand.max_column + 1):\n    v = ws_demand.cell(row=header_row, column=c).value\n    if isinstance(v, str) and v.strip().lower() == 'phase':\n        phase_col = c\n    if isinstance(v, str) and 'ductwork demand' in v.lower():\n        demand_col = c\n\nif phase_col is None or demand_col is None:\n    raise RuntimeError(f'Could not identify phase/demand columns (phase_col={phase_col}, demand_col={demand_col})')\n\n# Build mapping: phase_number -> demand_value for phases 8..56\nphases = list(range(8, 57))  # 8 to 56 inclusive = 49 phases\n\ndemand = {p: 0.0 for p in phases}\nfor r in range(header_row + 1, ws_demand.max_row + 1):\n    p_val = ws_demand.cell(row=r, column=phase_col).value\n    d_val = ws_demand.cell(row=r, column=demand_col).value\n\n    if p_val is None:\n        continue\n\n    try:\n        p = int(p_val)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        continue\n\n    if 8 <= p <= 56:\n        demand[p] = float(d_val) if d_val is not None else 0.0\n\nprint(f'Header row: {header_row}, phase_col: {phase_col}, demand_col: {demand_col}')\nprint('Demand values:')\nfor p in phases:\n    print(f'  Phase {p}: {demand[p]}')\n\nwb_demand.close()\n\n# --- 2. Compute schedule for phases 8..56 ---\n# Initial condition: at Phase 8, Calc Start + Scheduled Demand = 1138.66\n# Start of Phase Past Due = max(0, calc_start)\n# Since calc_start for phase 8 will be >= 0 here, we can set:\n#   calc_start_8 = 1138.66 - demand[8]\ninitial_total = 1138.66\n\nresults = []  # list of dicts\nprev_end_backlog = None\n\nfor phase in phases:\n    sched_demand = demand.get(phase, 0.0)\n\n    if phase == 8:\n        calc_start = initial_total - sched_demand\n    else:\n        calc_start = prev_end_backlog\n\n    start_past_due = max(0.0, calc_start)\n\n    # Choose Days Worked\n    if start_past_due > 0.01:\n        # Try smallest in {5, 6} such that calc_start + demand - 35*days <= 0\n        chosen = None\n        for d in [5, 6]:\n            if calc_start + sched_demand - 35.0 * d <= 0:\n                chosen = d\n                break\n        if chosen is None:\n            chosen = 6\n        days_worked = chosen\n    else:\n        # start_past_due <= 0.01\n        if sched_demand <= 140:\n            days_worked = 4\n        else:\n            days_worked = 5\n\n    weekly_capacity = 35.0 * days_worked\n    end_backlog = calc_start + sched_demand - weekly_capacity\n    overtime = 10.0 * max(0, days_worked - 4)\n\n    results.append({\n        'Phase': phase,\n        'Days Worked': days_worked,\n        'Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)': round(sched_demand, 2),\n        'Weekly Capacity (Std Hrs)': round(weekly_capacity, 2),\n        'Start of Phase Past Due (Std Hrs)': round(start_past_due, 2),\n        'End of Phase Backlog/Buffer (Std Hrs)': round(end_backlog, 2),\n        'Overtime Hours': round(overtime, 2),\n    })\n\n    prev_end_backlog = end_backlog\n\n# Print for verification\nprint('\\nComputed results:')\nfor r in results:\n    print(r)\n\n# Verify phase 8 initial condition\nr8 = results[0]\nphase8_sum = r8['Start of Phase Past Due (Std Hrs)'] + r8['Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)']\nprint(f\"\\nPhase 8 check: Start Past Due ({r8['Start of Phase Past Due (Std Hrs)']}) + Demand ({r8['Scheduled Demand (Std Hrs)']}) = {phase8_sum}\")\nprint(f\"Expected: {initial_total}\")\n\n# --- 3.
